# In pursuance of Regulation 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations 1988, it is hereby notified that in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 20 1 c of the Chartered Accountants Act

NOTIFICATION New Delhi, the 20th July, 2023 (Chartered Accountants) 3WCA (4)/1/2019-20.—In pursuance of Regulation 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations 1988, it is hereby notified that in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 20(1) (c) of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, the Council of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India has removed from the Register of Members of this Institute with effect from 01/03/2020 on account of non-payment of prescribed fees, the names of the following Sr. No.
